            "♨️ [GIBBS_MAXIMUM_ENTROPY_HEAT_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "🌡️ [E58:{}] FREE_F:{:+.2} | ENTROPY_S:{:.3} | WORK_BIAS:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, free_energy_f, entropy_s, work_bias, thermo_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, temp_t, e_vec);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_59_tsallis_q_entropy.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector4;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, rolling_var, q_index, prob_vec4)
static TSALLIS_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, f64, f64, Vector4<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, 1.0, 1.35, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, 1.0, 1.35, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, 1.0, 1.35, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
]);

// ENGINE 59: Tier-0 Tsallis Non-Extensive q-Entropy & Power-Law q-Gaussian Cascade Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = TSALLIS_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_var, prev_q, prev_p) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, 1.0, 1.35, prev_p);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let sq_ret = ret_bps * ret_bps;
        let sigma_sq = ((prev_var * 0.88) + (sq_ret * 0.12)).max(0.05);

        // Estimate Tsallis Entropic Index q from excess kurtosis bursts (q -> 1 is Gaussian, q > 1.5 is heavy power-law)
        let tail_ratio = (sq_ret / sigma_sq).clamp(0.1, 12.0);
        let inst_q = (1.10 + (0.12 * tail_ratio)).clamp(1.05, 2.40);
        let q_idx = (prev_q * 0.85) + (inst_q * 0.15);

        // q-Gaussian Weights across 4 state attractors [-2.0, -0.6, +0.6, +2.0] via Tsallis q-exponential
        let states = Vector4::new(-2.0, -0.6, 0.6, 2.0);
        let beta = 1.0 / (2.0 * sigma_sq);
        let q_weights = states.map(|s| {
            let u = beta * (s - ret_bps).powi(2);
            let base = (1.0 - (1.0 - q_idx) * u).max(1e-4);
            base.powf(1.0 / (1.0 - q_idx))
        });

        let norm_p = q_weights / q_weights.sum().max(1e-6);
        let smooth_p = (prev_p * 0.72) + (norm_p * 0.28);

        // Tsallis Non-Extensive Entropy: S_q = (1 - sum(P_i^q)) / (q - 1)
        let sum_p_q = smooth_p.map(|p| p.powf(q_idx)).sum();
        let tsallis_s_q = (1.0 - sum_p_q) / (q_idx - 1.0);
        let tail_skew = (smooth_p[2] + smooth_p[3]) - (smooth_p[0] + smooth_p[1]);

        let tsallis_regime = if q_idx > 1.52 && tail_skew > 0.28 {
            "⚡ [TSALLIS_Q_GAUSSIAN_BULL_AVALANCHE]"
        } else if q_idx > 1.52 && tail_skew < -0.28 {
            "🩸 [TSALLIS_Q_GAUSSIAN_BEAR_CASCADE]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [TSALLIS_EXTENSIVE_GAUSSIAN_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "🌪️ [E59:{}] q_INDEX:{:.3} | S_q_ENTROPY:{:.3} | q_SKEW:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, q_idx, tsallis_s_q, tail_skew, tsallis_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, sigma_sq, q_idx, smooth_p);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_60_renyi_multifractal.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector4;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, prob_vec4[deep_dn, mild_dn, mild_up, deep_up])
static RENYI_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector4<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
]);

// ENGINE 60: Tier-0 Renyi Generalized Multi-Fractal Entropy Spectrum & Collision Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = RENYI_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_p) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_p);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;

        // Soft-assign micro-return across 4 fractal velocity states [-2.0, -0.5, +0.5, +2.0]
        let centers = Vector4::new(-2.0, -0.5, 0.5, 2.0);
        let raw_w = centers.map(|c| (-0.45 * (ret_bps - c).powi(2)).exp());
        let inst_p = raw_w / raw_w.sum().max(1e-6);

        let mut p_vec = (prev_p * 0.78) + (inst_p * 0.22);
        p_vec /= p_vec.sum().max(1e-6);

        // Renyi Entropy of order alpha: H_alpha = (1 / (1 - alpha)) * ln( sum(P_i^alpha) )
        // Order alpha = 0.5 (Rare Tail-Support Spectrum)
        let sum_a05 = p_vec.map(|p| p.max(1e-6).sqrt()).sum();
        let h_05 = (1.0 / (1.0 - 0.5)) * sum_a05.ln();

        // Order alpha = 2.0 (Quadratic Collision Entropy: H_2 = -ln sum(P_i^2))
        let sum_a2 = p_vec.dot(&p_vec).max(1e-6);
        let h_2 = -sum_a2.ln();

        // Order alpha = 4.0 (High-Concentration Singularity Spectrum)
        let sum_a4 = p_vec.map(|p| p.powi(4)).sum().max(1e-9);
        let h_4 = (1.0 / (1.0 - 4.0)) * sum_a4.ln();

        // Multi-Fractal Spectrum Width: Delta_H = H_0.5 - H_4 & Directional Singularity Bias
        let spectrum_width = (h_05 - h_4).max(0.0);
        let dir_bias = (p_vec[2] + p_vec[3]) - (p_vec[0] + p_vec[1]);

        let renyi_regime = if spectrum_width > 0.35 && dir_bias > 0.30 {
            "💎 [RENYI_MULTIFRACTAL_BULL_SINGULARITY]"
        } else if spectrum_width > 0.35 && dir_bias < -0.30 {
            "💎 [RENYI_MULTIFRACTAL_BEAR_SINGULARITY]"
        } else {
            "🌀 [RENYI_MONOFRACTAL_DISPERSION]"
        };

        println!(
            "🔬 [E60:{}] H2_COLL:{:.3} | ΔH_SPEC:{:.3} | BIAS:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, h_2, spectrum_width, dir_bias, renyi_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, p_vec);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_61_kl_divergence_shift.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector4;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, fast_posterior_p, slow_prior_q)
static KL_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector4<f64>, Vector4<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25), Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25), Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25), Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
]);

// ENGINE 61: Tier-0 Kullback-Leibler (KL) Divergence & Jeffreys Relative Entropy Shift Detector
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = KL_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_p, prev_q) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_p, prev_q);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;

        // Soft-map micro-return across 4 canonical regime bins [-2.0, -0.5, +0.5, +2.0]
        let centers = Vector4::new(-2.0, -0.5, 0.5, 2.0);
        let raw_w = centers.map(|c| (-0.50 * (ret_bps - c).powi(2)).exp());
        let inst_dist = raw_w / raw_w.sum().max(1e-6);

        // Fast Posterior P (reacts to immediate shock) vs Slow Prior Q (baseline memory)
        let mut p_fast = (prev_p * 0.60) + (inst_dist * 0.40);
        let mut q_slow = (prev_q * 0.94) + (inst_dist * 0.06);
        p_fast /= p_fast.sum().max(1e-6);
        q_slow /= q_slow.sum().max(1e-6);

        // Forward KL: D_KL(P || Q) = sum( P_i * ln(P_i / Q_i) ) & Reverse KL: D_KL(Q || P)
        let mut d_kl_pq = 0.0;
        let mut d_kl_qp = 0.0;
        for i in 0..4 {
            let pi = p_fast[i].max(1e-6);
            let qi = q_slow[i].max(1e-6);
            d_kl_pq += pi * (pi / qi).ln();
            d_kl_qp += qi * (qi / pi).ln();
        }

        // Jeffreys Symmetrized Divergence J_KL = D_KL(P || Q) + D_KL(Q || P)
        let jeffreys_div = (d_kl_pq + d_kl_qp).max(0.0);
        let info_dir = ((p_fast[2] + p_fast[3]) - (q_slow[2] + q_slow[3])) * 2.0;

        let kl_regime = if jeffreys_div > 0.12 && info_dir > 0.10 {
            "🚀 [KL_INFO_SHOCK_BULL_REGIME_SHIFT]"
        } else if jeffreys_div > 0.12 && info_dir < -0.10 {
            "🩸 [KL_INFO_SHOCK_BEAR_REGIME_SHIFT]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [KL_PRIOR_POSTERIOR_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "📡 [E61:{}] D_KL(P||Q):{:.4} | J_SYM:{:.4} | ΔINFO:{:+.3} -> {}",
            symbol, d_kl_pq, jeffreys_div, info_dir, kl_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, p_fast, q_slow);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_62_wasserstein_transport.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector4;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, fast_mass_p, slow_mass_q)
static WASSERSTEIN_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector4<f64>, Vector4<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25), Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25), Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
    (0.0, Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25), Vector4::new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25)),
]);

// ENGINE 62: Tier-0 Wasserstein Optimal Transport & Earth Mover's Distance (EMD) Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = WASSERSTEIN_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_p, prev_q) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_p, prev_q);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let book_skew = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);
        let combined_impulse = ret_bps + (book_skew * 0.8);

        // Project impulse onto 4 metric space grid nodes [-2.0, -0.6, +0.6, +2.0]
        let grid = Vector4::new(-2.0, -0.6, 0.6, 2.0);
        let raw_w = grid.map(|g| (-0.45 * (combined_impulse - g).powi(2)).exp());
        let inst_mass = raw_w / raw_w.sum().max(1e-6);

        let mut p_fast = (prev_p * 0.58) + (inst_mass * 0.42);
        let mut q_slow = (prev_q * 0.93) + (inst_mass * 0.07);
        p_fast /= p_fast.sum().max(1e-6);
        q_slow /= q_slow.sum().max(1e-6);

        // Kantorovich-Rubinstein 1D Closed-Form Wasserstein Distance: W_1(P, Q) = sum |CDF_P(i) - CDF_Q(i)| * dx
        let mut cdf_p = 0.0;
        let mut cdf_q = 0.0;
        let mut w1_emd = 0.0;
        let mut signed_flux = 0.0;
        for i in 0..3 {
            cdf_p += p_fast[i];
            cdf_q += q_slow[i];
            let step_dx = grid[i + 1] - grid[i];
            let diff_cdf = cdf_q - cdf_p; // Positive when P shifts mass rightward (bullish) relative to Q
            w1_emd += diff_cdf.abs() * step_dx;
            signed_flux += diff_cdf * step_dx;
        }

        let ot_regime = if w1_emd > 0.22 && signed_flux > 0.15 {
            "🚜 [WASSERSTEIN_BULL_MASS_TRANSPORT]"
        } else if w1_emd > 0.22 && signed_flux < -0.15 {
            "🚜 [WASSERSTEIN_BEAR_MASS_TRANSPORT]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [WASSERSTEIN_ZERO_WORK_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "🚛 [E62:{}] W1_EMD:{:.4} | OT_FLUX:{:+.4} | BOOK_SKEW:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, w1_emd, signed_flux, book_skew, ot_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, p_fast, q_slow);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_63_fisher_information.rs =====
use nalgebra::{Matrix2, Vector2};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, theta_vec[mean_mu, var_sigma2])
static FISHER_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector2<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector2::new(0.0, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector2::new(0.0, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector2::new(0.0, 1.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 63: Tier-0 Fisher Information Metric & Cramer-Rao Riemannian Manifold Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = FISHER_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_theta) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_theta);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;

        // Parameter manifold coordinates theta = [mu, sigma^2]
        let mu = (prev_theta[0] * 0.84) + (ret_bps * 0.16);
        let diff = ret_bps - mu;
        let sigma_sq = ((prev_theta[1] * 0.85) + (diff * diff * 0.15)).clamp(0.05, 25.0);

        let next_theta = Vector2::new(mu, sigma_sq);
        let d_theta = next_theta - prev_theta;

        // Fisher Information Metric Tensor G(theta) = diag(1 / sigma^2, 1 / (2 * sigma^4))
        let g11 = 1.0 / sigma_sq;
        let g22 = 1.0 / (2.0 * sigma_sq * sigma_sq);
        let fisher_g = Matrix2::new(g11, 0.0, 0.0, g22);

        // Squared Riemannian Geodesic Distance ds^2 = d_theta^T * G * d_theta
        let ds_sq = d_theta.dot(&(fisher_g * d_theta));
        let fisher_speed = ds_sq.sqrt();

        // Cramer-Rao Lower Bound Trace: Tr(G^-1) = sigma^2 + 2 * sigma^4
        let crlb_bound = sigma_sq + (2.0 * sigma_sq * sigma_sq);

        let fisher_regime = if fisher_speed > 0.35 && mu > 0.25 {
            "📐 [FISHER_GEODESIC_WARP_BULL_BREAKOUT]"
        } else if fisher_speed > 0.35 && mu < -0.25 {
            "📐 [FISHER_GEODESIC_WARP_BEAR_BREAKOUT]"
        } else if crlb_bound < 0.80 {
            "🔬 [CRAMER_RAO_HYPER_PRECISION_LOCK]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [FISHER_MANIFOLD_FLAT_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "🧭 [E63:{}] ds_FISHER:{:.4} | CRLB_VAR:{:.2} | μ_MANIFOLD:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, fisher_speed, crlb_bound, next_theta[0], fisher_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_theta);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_64_schrodinger_tunneling.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, q_vec[kinetic_e, barrier_v, tunnel_prob])
static SCHRODINGER_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 1.5, 0.1)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 1.5, 0.1)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 1.5, 0.1)),
]);

// ENGINE 64: Tier-0 Schrodinger Wavefunction & WKB Quantum Barrier Tunneling Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let best_bid = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("best_bid"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(price);
    let best_ask = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("best_ask"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(price);
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = SCHRODINGER_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_q) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_q);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let spread_width = (((best_ask - best_bid).max(0.0) / price) * 10000.0).clamp(0.1, 10.0);

        // Wavepacket Kinetic Energy E = 0.5 * m * v^2 & Opposing Orderbook Potential Barrier V_0
        let inst_e = (0.5 * ret_bps * ret_bps).clamp(0.02, 25.0);
        let opposing_wall = if ret_bps >= 0.0 { ask_sz } else { bid_sz };
        let inst_v = (opposing_wall.max(1.0).ln() * 0.85).clamp(0.20, 15.0);

        let kin_e = (prev_q[0] * 0.78) + (inst_e * 0.22);
        let bar_v = (prev_q[1] * 0.85) + (inst_v * 0.15);

        // WKB Quantum Tunneling Transmission Coefficient: T = exp( -2 * width * sqrt(max(V_0 - E, 0)) )
        let barrier_deficit = (bar_v - kin_e).max(0.0);
        let wkb_exponent = (-0.45 * spread_width.sqrt() * barrier_deficit.sqrt()).clamp(-10.0, 0.0);
        let inst_tunnel = if kin_e >= bar_v { 0.99 } else { wkb_exponent.exp() };

        let tunnel_p = (prev_q[2] * 0.72) + (inst_tunnel * 0.28);
        let next_q = Vector3::new(kin_e, bar_v, tunnel_p);

        let q_regime = if tunnel_p > 0.62 && ret_bps > 0.15 {
            "⚛️ [QUANTUM_TUNNELING_BULL_WALL_BREACH]"
        } else if tunnel_p > 0.62 && ret_bps < -0.15 {
            "⚛️ [QUANTUM_TUNNELING_BEAR_WALL_BREACH]"
        } else if tunnel_p < 0.25 {
            "🧱 [WAVEFUNCTION_BARRIER_REFLECTION]"
        } else {
            "〰️ [SCHRODINGER_COHERENT_WAVE_PACKET]"
        };

        println!(
            "🌌 [E64:{}] E_KIN:{:.2} | V_WALL:{:.2} | P(TUNNEL):{:.1}% -> {}",
            symbol, next_q[0], next_q[1], next_q[2] * 100.0, q_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_q);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_65_heisenberg_uncertainty.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, h_vec[delta_x_pos, delta_p_mom, uncertainty_product])
static HEISENBERG_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 65: Tier-0 Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle & Phase-Space Minimum Wavepacket Bound Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = HEISENBERG_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_h) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_h);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let abs_ret = ret_bps.abs();

        // Position dispersion delta_x (Price volatility envelope) & Momentum dispersion delta_p (Velocity variance)
        let delta_x = ((prev_h[0] * 0.84) + (abs_ret * 0.16)).clamp(0.05, 50.0);
        let velocity_v = ret_bps;
        let delta_p = ((prev_h[1] * 0.84) + (velocity_v.abs() * 0.16)).clamp(0.05, 50.0);

        // Heisenberg Phase-Space Action Uncertainty Product: Delta_x * Delta_p >= hbar_effective / 2
        let hbar_effective = 0.50; // Canonical scaled quantum action constant for trading phase-space
        let inst_product = delta_x * delta_p;
        let uncertainty_prod = (prev_h[2] * 0.75) + (inst_product * 0.25);

        // Coherence compression ratio relative to the quantum minimum bound
        let bound_ratio = uncertainty_prod / (hbar_effective * 0.5);
        let next_h = Vector3::new(delta_x, delta_p, uncertainty_prod);

        let heisenberg_regime = if bound_ratio < 1.25 && ret_bps > 0.30 {
            "⚛️ [HEISENBERG_MIN_UNCERTAINTY_BULL_SQUEEZE]"
        } else if bound_ratio < 1.25 && ret_bps < -0.30 {
            "⚛️ [HEISENBERG_MIN_UNCERTAINTY_BEAR_CRASH]"
        } else if bound_ratio > 4.50 {
            "🌫️ [HEISENBERG_HIGH_ENTROPY_THERMAL_NOISE]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [HEISENBERG_COHERENT_WAVEPACKET_DRIFT]"
        };

        println!(
            "📐 [E65:{}] ΔX:{:.2} | ΔP:{:.2} | PROD:{:.3} (Ratio:{:.2}) -> {}",
            symbol, next_h[0], next_h[1], next_h[2], bound_ratio, heisenberg_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_h);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_66_quantum_harmonic_osc.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (anchor_price, qho_vec[disp_x, mom_p, quantum_level_n])
static QHO_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 66: Tier-0 Quantum Harmonic Oscillator, Dirac Ladder Operators & Ground-State Well Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = QHO_STATE.lock() {
        let (mut anchor_p, prev_qho) = state[idx];

        if anchor_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_qho);
            return;
        }

        // Displacement x from dynamic harmonic well center & canonical momentum p
        let disp_x = ((price - anchor_p) / anchor_p) * 10000.0;
        let mom_p = disp_x - prev_qho[0];
        anchor_p = (anchor_p * 0.92) + (price * 0.08);

        // Harmonic well stiffness omega derived from orderbook restoring depth
        let book_stiffness = ((bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0).ln() * 0.35).clamp(0.40, 3.0);
        let omega = book_stiffness;
        let hbar = 0.50;

        // Dirac Ladder Operator occupation number: n = (m*omega*x^2 + p^2/(m*omega)) / (2*hbar) - 0.5
        let hamiltonian_ratio = ((omega * disp_x * disp_x) + (mom_p * mom_p / omega)) / (2.0 * hbar);
        let inst_n = (hamiltonian_ratio - 0.5).clamp(0.0, 15.0);
        let smooth_n = (prev_qho[2] * 0.72) + (inst_n * 0.28);

        // Quantized Eigen-Energy E_n = hbar * omega * (n + 0.5)
        let energy_en = hbar * omega * (smooth_n + 0.5);
        let next_qho = Vector3::new(disp_x, mom_p, smooth_n);

        let qho_regime = if smooth_n > 2.4 && disp_x > 0.35 {
            "🚀 [QHO_LADDER_EXCITATION_BULL_ESCAPE]"
        } else if smooth_n > 2.4 && disp_x < -0.35 {
            "🩸 [QHO_LADDER_EXCITATION_BEAR_ESCAPE]"
        } else if smooth_n < 0.45 {
            "🧊 [QHO_ZERO_POINT_GROUND_STATE_COIL]"
        } else {
            "〰️ [QHO_BOUND_HARMONIC_OSCILLATION]"
        };

        println!(
            "⚛️ [E66:{}] LEVEL_n:{:.2} | E_n:{:.2} | X_WELL:{:+.2}bps -> {}",
            symbol, next_qho[2], energy_en, next_qho[0], qho_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (anchor_p, next_qho);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_67_langevin_dynamics.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, lang_vec[vel_v, drag_gamma, temp_kbt])
static LANGEVIN_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.5, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.5, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.5, 1.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 67: Tier-0 Langevin Stochastic Differential Equation & Fluctuation-Dissipation Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = LANGEVIN_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_l) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_l);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let book_depth = (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0);

        // Viscous damping coefficient gamma proportional to passive orderbook thickness
        let inst_gamma = (book_depth.ln() * 0.22).clamp(0.10, 2.50);
        let gamma = (prev_l[1] * 0.82) + (inst_gamma * 0.18);

        // Langevin velocity update: dv = -gamma * v * dt + external_shock
        let v_prev = prev_l[0];
        let dv = (-gamma * v_prev * 0.25) + (ret_bps * 0.65);
        let v_next = (v_prev * 0.60) + (dv * 0.40);

        // Fluctuation-Dissipation Theorem: Thermal Energy k_B*T = m * E[v^2] & Einstein Diffusion D = k_B*T / gamma
        let inst_kbt = (ret_bps * ret_bps).max(0.05);
        let kbt = ((prev_l[2] * 0.85) + (inst_kbt * 0.15)).clamp(0.05, 30.0);
        let einstein_diff_d = kbt / gamma;

        let next_l = Vector3::new(v_next, gamma, kbt);

        let lang_regime = if v_next > 0.55 && einstein_diff_d > 1.8 {
            "🚀 [LANGEVIN_UNDERDAMPED_BALLISTIC_BULL]"
        } else if v_next < -0.55 && einstein_diff_d > 1.8 {
            "🩸 [LANGEVIN_UNDERDAMPED_BALLISTIC_BEAR]"
        } else if gamma > 1.25 && v_next.abs() < 0.25 {
            "🍯 [LANGEVIN_OVERDAMPED_VISCOUS_TRAP]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [FLUCTUATION_DISSIPATION_BALANCE]"
        };

        println!(
            "🧪 [E67:{}] V_LANG:{:+.2} | γ_DRAG:{:.2} | D_EINSTEIN:{:.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_l[0], next_l[1], einstein_diff_d, lang_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_l);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_68_ornstein_uhlenbeck.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (anchor_mu, prev_spread_x, ou_vec[theta_speed, sigma_eq, ou_zscore])
static OU_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, 0.0, Vector3::new(0.25, 1.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, 0.0, Vector3::new(0.25, 1.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, 0.0, Vector3::new(0.25, 1.0, 0.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 68: Tier-0 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) Mean-Reverting Velocity & Half-Life Decay Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = OU_STATE.lock() {
        let (mut mu_anchor, prev_x, prev_ou) = state[idx];

        if mu_anchor <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, 0.0, prev_ou);
            return;
        }

        // Deviation from dynamic equilibrium anchor in basis points: X_t = ((P_t - mu) / mu) * 10000
        let x_curr = ((price - mu_anchor) / mu_anchor) * 10000.0;
        let dx = x_curr - prev_x;
        mu_anchor = (mu_anchor * 0.94) + (price * 0.06);

        // Estimate OU Mean-Reversion Stiffness theta from restoring pull (-dx / prev_x)
        let inst_pull = if prev_x.abs() > 0.15 {
            (-dx / prev_x).clamp(0.02, 1.50)
        } else {
            prev_ou[0]
        };
        let theta = ((prev_ou[0] * 0.86) + (inst_pull * 0.14)).clamp(0.05, 1.20);

        // OU Stationary Equilibrium Volatility: sigma_eq = sigma_diff / sqrt(2 * theta)
        let resid_shock = (dx + (theta * prev_x)).abs();
        let sigma_diff = ((prev_ou[1] * 0.85) + (resid_shock * 0.15)).clamp(0.10, 20.0);
        let sigma_eq = (sigma_diff / (2.0 * theta).sqrt()).max(0.10);

        // Exact Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Stationary Z-Score & Half-Life tau_1/2 = ln(2) / theta
        let ou_z = (x_curr / sigma_eq).clamp(-5.0, 5.0);
        let half_life = std::f64::consts::LN_2 / theta;
        let next_ou = Vector3::new(theta, sigma_diff, ou_z);

        let ou_regime = if ou_z < -1.85 && theta > 0.18 {
            "🟢 [OU_OVERSOLD_ELASTIC_SNAPBACK_LONG]"
        } else if ou_z > 1.85 && theta > 0.18 {
            "🔴 [OU_OVERBOUGHT_ELASTIC_SNAPBACK_SHORT]"
        } else if theta < 0.09 && ou_z.abs() > 2.2 {
            "⚡ [OU_HALFLIFE_BLOWOUT_TREND_ESCAPE]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [OU_STATIONARY_EQUILIBRIUM_ORBIT]"
        };

        println!(
            "🧲 [E68:{}] OU_Z:{:+.2} | θ_PULL:{:.3} | τ_HALF:{:.1}tks -> {}",
            symbol, next_ou[2], next_ou[0], half_life, ou_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (mu_anchor, x_curr, next_ou);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_69_kramers_escape.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (anchor_p, kramers_vec[state_x, noise_d, escape_rate_k])

        return;
    }

        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = KRAMERS_STATE.lock() {
        let (mut anchor_p, prev_k) = state[idx];

        if anchor_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_k);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - anchor_p) / anchor_p) * 10000.0;
        anchor_p = (anchor_p * 0.93) + (price * 0.07);

        // Normalized reaction coordinate x in bistable potential V(x) = 0.25*a*x^4 - 0.5*b*x^2
        let x_coord = ((prev_k[0] * 0.75) + (ret_bps * 0.25)).clamp(-3.5, 3.5);
        let noise_d = ((prev_k[1] * 0.85) + ((ret_bps * ret_bps) * 0.15)).clamp(0.10, 25.0);

        // Double-well barrier height Delta_V = b^2 / (4*a) modulated by orderbook depth resistance
        let book_resist = ((bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0).ln() * 0.30).clamp(0.30, 3.0);
        let delta_v = (1.20 * book_resist).max(0.20);

        // Kramers Eyring-Kramers Transition Rate: k_esc = prefactor * exp(-Delta_V / D)
        let curvature_prefactor = 0.65;
        let inst_k = curvature_prefactor * (-delta_v / noise_d).exp();
        let k_esc = (prev_k[2] * 0.72) + (inst_k * 0.28);
        let mfpt_ticks = 1.0 / k_esc.max(1e-4);

        let next_k = Vector3::new(x_coord, noise_d, k_esc);

        let kramers_regime = if k_esc > 0.38 && x_coord > 0.40 {
            "🚀 [KRAMERS_SADDLE_CROSSING_BULL_WELL_TRANSITION]"
        } else if k_esc > 0.38 && x_coord < -0.40 {
            "🩸 [KRAMERS_SADDLE_CROSSING_BEAR_WELL_TRANSITION]"
        } else if k_esc < 0.12 {
            "🔒 [KRAMERS_METASTABLE_WELL_TRAPPED]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [KRAMERS_BISTABLE_BARRIER_FLUCTUATION]"
        };

        println!(
            "⛰️ [E69:{}] k_ESC:{:.3} | ΔV_BARR:{:.2} | MFPT:{:.1}tks | X:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_k[2], delta_v, mfpt_ticks, next_k[0], kramers_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (anchor_p, next_k);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_70_landau_ginzburg.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, lg_vec[order_phi, temp_r, free_energy_f])
static LANDAU_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.5, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.5, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.5, 0.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 70: Tier-0 Landau-Ginzburg Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking & Order-Parameter Field Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = LANDAU_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_lg) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_lg);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let h_field = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);

        // Order parameter phi (normalized directional alignment) & gradient d_phi
        let inst_phi = ((ret_bps * 0.8) + (h_field * 1.2)).tanh();
        let phi = (prev_lg[0] * 0.72) + (inst_phi * 0.28);
        let grad_phi = phi - prev_lg[0];

        // Landau Reduced Temperature r = alpha * (T - T_c): r < 0 triggers Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking
        let directional_coherence = phi.abs();
        let inst_r = 0.65 - (1.80 * directional_coherence);
        let r_param = ((prev_lg[1] * 0.82) + (inst_r * 0.18)).clamp(-1.50, 1.50);

        // Landau-Ginzburg Free Energy Density: F = 0.5*r*phi^2 + 0.25*beta*phi^4 + 0.5*kappa*(grad_phi)^2 - h*phi
        let beta = 1.20;
        let kappa = 0.80;
        let free_f = (0.5 * r_param * phi * phi)
            + (0.25 * beta * phi.powi(4))
            + (0.5 * kappa * grad_phi * grad_phi)
            - (0.40 * h_field * phi);

        let next_lg = Vector3::new(phi, r_param, free_f);

        let lg_regime = if r_param < -0.15 && phi > 0.45 {
            "⚡ [LANDAU_SYMMETRY_BROKEN_BULL_CONDENSATE]"
        } else if r_param < -0.15 && phi < -0.45 {
            "🩸 [LANDAU_SYMMETRY_BROKEN_BEAR_CONDENSATE]"
        } else if r_param.abs() <= 0.15 {
            "🌋 [GINZBURG_CRITICAL_OPALESCENCE_POINT]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [LANDAU_SYMMETRIC_DISORDERED_PHASE]"
        };

        println!(
            "👑 [E70:{}] φ_ORDER:{:+.3} | r_LANDAU:{:+.3} | F_LG:{:+.3} -> {}",
            symbol, next_lg[0], next_lg[1], next_lg[2], lg_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_lg);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_71_renormalization_group.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, rg_vec[coupling_g, beta_beta, scale_xi])
static RG_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 0.0, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 0.0, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 0.0, 1.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 71: Tier-0 Renormalization Group (RG) Flow & Scale-Invariant Criticality Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = RG_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_rg) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_rg);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let abs_ret = ret_bps.abs();

        // Effective coupling constant g (non-linear micro-feedback intensity)
        let inst_g = (prev_rg[0] * 0.82) + (abs_ret * 0.18);
        let g_coup = inst_g.clamp(0.05, 5.0);

        // Wilsonian Beta Function dg/d(ln b) = -b*g^2 + c*g^3 (Non-trivial fixed point at beta(g) = 0)
        let beta_beta = (-1.20 * g_coup * g_coup) + (0.45 * g_coup * g_coup * g_coup);
        let smoothed_beta = (prev_rg[1] * 0.78) + (beta_beta * 0.22);

        // Correlation length xi diverging near critical fixed point: xi ~ |g - g_star|^(-nu)
        let fixed_point = 0.65;
        let distance_to_fp = (g_coup - fixed_point).abs().max(0.02);
        let scale_xi = (1.0 / distance_to_fp).clamp(0.5, 50.0);

        let next_rg = Vector3::new(g_coup, smoothed_beta, scale_xi);

        let rg_regime = if scale_xi > 15.0 && smoothed_beta < -0.10 {
            "🌐 [RG_SCALE_INVARIANT_CRITICAL_AVALANCHE]"
        } else if g_coup > 2.20 {
            "🔥 [RG_ULTRAVIOLET_LANDAU_POLE_BLOWOUT]"
        } else if scale_xi < 1.5 {
            "⚖️ [RG_IR_STABLE_GAUSSIAN_FIXED_POINT]"
        } else {
            "🌀 [RG_RENORMALIZATION_FLOW_TRANSITION]"
        };

        println!(
            "📐 [E71:{}] COUPLING_g:{:.3} | β(g):{:.4} | ξ_CORR:{:.1} -> {}",
            symbol, next_rg[0], next_rg[1], next_rg[2], rg_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_rg);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_72_topological_hall.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, d_vec[dx_momentum, dy_book_flow, dz_mass_gap])
static TOPO_HALL_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.1, 0.1, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.1, 0.1, 1.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.1, 0.1, 1.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 72: Tier-0 Topological Insulator, Berry Curvature & Quantum Hall Edge-State Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = TOPO_HALL_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_d) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_d);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let book_imb = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);

        // Bloch Hamiltonian vector d(k) = [dx, dy, dz]: dz < 0 triggers Topological Band Inversion
        let dx = (prev_d[0] * 0.75) + (ret_bps * 0.25);
        let dy = (prev_d[1] * 0.75) + ((book_imb * 2.0) * 0.25);
        let inst_gap = 0.85 - (ret_bps.abs() * 0.55) - (book_imb.abs() * 0.65);
        let dz = ((prev_d[2] * 0.82) + (inst_gap * 0.18)).clamp(-2.5, 2.5);

        let next_d = Vector3::new(dx, dy, dz);
        let norm_d = next_d.norm().max(1e-4);

        // Berry Curvature Flux Omega_z = (d . (prev_d x next_d)) / |d|^3 & Quantized Hall Conductivity
        let cross_flow = prev_d.cross(&next_d);
        let berry_omega = next_d.dot(&cross_flow) / (norm_d * norm_d * norm_d);
        let chern_proxy = if dz < 0.0 { dx.signum() } else { 0.0 };
        let hall_sigma_xy = (chern_proxy * 0.70) + (berry_omega.clamp(-1.5, 1.5) * 0.30);

        let topo_regime = if dz < -0.10 && hall_sigma_xy > 0.45 {
            "⚡ [TOPO_CHIRAL_EDGE_STATE_BULL_SUPERCURRENT]"
        } else if dz < -0.10 && hall_sigma_xy < -0.45 {
            "🩸 [TOPO_CHIRAL_EDGE_STATE_BEAR_SUPERCURRENT]"
        } else if dz > 0.35 {
            "🧱 [TOPO_BULK_INSULATOR_GAPPED_CHOP]"
        } else {
            "🌀 [DIRAC_CONE_GAP_CLOSING_TRANSITION]"
        };

        println!(
            "🍩 [E72:{}] σ_XY_HALL:{:+.3} | Δ_GAP(dz):{:+.3} | Ω_BERRY:{:+.4} -> {}",
            symbol, hall_sigma_xy, next_d[2], berry_omega, topo_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_d);
    }
}

use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, q_stat_vec[temp_t, chem_mu, bec_fraction])
static BOSE_FERMI_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, -0.5, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, -0.5, 0.0)),

// ENGINE 73: Tier-0 Fermi-Dirac Pauli Exclusion & Bose-Einstein Liquidity Condensation Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {

    }

    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {

    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {

    } else {

    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

        return;
    }

        let (last_p, prev_q) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_q);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let book_imb = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);

        // Effective thermal dispersion T & Chemical Potential mu -> 0_minus (approaching ground state epsilon_0 = 0)
        let inst_t = (ret_bps.abs() * 0.6 + (1.0 - book_imb.abs()) * 0.8).clamp(0.10, 4.0);
        let temp_t = (prev_q[0] * 0.82) + (inst_t * 0.18);

        // High directional crowding drives chemical potential mu toward 0 from below (-0.02)
        let crowding = (ret_bps.abs() * 0.45) + (book_imb.abs() * 0.85);
        let inst_mu = (-1.20 / (1.0 + crowding * 2.5)).clamp(-1.50, -0.02);
        let chem_mu = (prev_q[1] * 0.78) + (inst_mu * 0.22);

        // Ground-state (epsilon_0 = 0) Bose-Einstein occupation N_0 vs Fermi-Dirac Pauli Pressure N_F
        let x_arg = (-chem_mu / temp_t).clamp(0.02, 10.0);
        let bose_n0 = 1.0 / (x_arg.exp() - 1.0).max(0.02);
        let fermi_nf = 1.0 / (x_arg.exp() + 1.0);

        let bec_ratio = (bose_n0 / (bose_n0 + fermi_nf + 2.0)).clamp(0.0, 0.99);
        let smooth_bec = (prev_q[2] * 0.72) + (bec_ratio * 0.28);
        let next_q = Vector3::new(temp_t, chem_mu, smooth_bec);

        let q_regime = if smooth_bec > 0.62 && (ret_bps + book_imb) > 0.25 {
            "⚡ [BOSE_EINSTEIN_CONDENSATE_BULL_SUPERFLUID]"
        } else if smooth_bec > 0.62 && (ret_bps + book_imb) < -0.25 {
            "🩸 [BOSE_EINSTEIN_CONDENSATE_BEAR_AVALANCHE]"
        } else if fermi_nf > 0.42 && smooth_bec < 0.35 {
            "🧱 [FERMI_DIRAC_PAULI_EXCLUSION_QUEUE_LOCK]"
        } else {
            "♨️ [MAXWELL_BOLTZMANN_CLASSICAL_GAS]"
        };

        println!(
            "🧊 [E73:{}] BEC_f:{:.1}% | μ_CHEM:{:+.3} | FERMI_P:{:.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_q[2] * 100.0, next_q[1], fermi_nf, q_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_q);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_74_casimir_vacuum_force.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, cas_vec[spread_d_bps, casimir_force, plate_asymmetry])
static CASIMIR_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 0.5, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 0.5, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 0.5, 0.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 74: Tier-0 Casimir Quantum Vacuum Fluctuation & Micro-Spread Zero-Point Force Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let best_bid = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("best_bid"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(price);
    let best_ask = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("best_ask"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(price);
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = CASIMIR_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_c) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_c);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;

        // Bid-Ask Cavity Separation Distance d (in scaled basis points, clamped to prevent 1/d^4 singularity)
        let raw_spread_bps = (((best_ask - best_bid).max(0.0) / price) * 10000.0).max(0.25);
        let spread_d = ((prev_c[0] * 0.78) + (raw_spread_bps * 0.22)).clamp(0.35, 10.0);

        // Casimir Zero-Point Attractive Pressure: F_cas = (pi^2 * hbar_c) / (240 * d^4) * Plate_Conductivity
        let plate_mass = ((bid_sz * ask_sz).max(1.0).ln() * 0.45).clamp(0.30, 4.0);
        let d_quartic = spread_d.powi(4).max(0.05);
        let inst_casimir = ((1.20 * plate_mass) / d_quartic).clamp(0.01, 25.0);
        let casimir_f = (prev_c[1] * 0.72) + (inst_casimir * 0.28);

        // Dynamic Casimir Directional Impulse when one boundary plate dominates
        let plate_imb = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);
        let asym_drive = (prev_c[2] * 0.75) + (((plate_imb * 0.6) + (ret_bps * 0.4)) * 0.25);

        let next_c = Vector3::new(spread_d, casimir_f, asym_drive);

        let casimir_regime = if casimir_f > 2.20 && asym_drive > 0.28 {
            "⚡ [CASIMIR_VACUUM_IMPLOSION_BULL_SNAP]"
        } else if casimir_f > 2.20 && asym_drive < -0.28 {
            "🩸 [CASIMIR_VACUUM_IMPLOSION_BEAR_SNAP]"
        } else if spread_d > 2.50 {
            "🌌 [CASIMIR_WIDE_CAVITY_LOW_PRESSURE]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [ZERO_POINT_VACUUM_FLUCTUATION_BALANCE]"
        };

        println!(
            "🧲 [E74:{}] F_CASIMIR:{:.3} | d_GAP:{:.2}bps | DRIVE:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_c[1], next_c[0], next_c[2], casimir_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_c);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_75_feynman_path_integral.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, path_actions_s[bear_s, neutral_s, bull_s], coherence_amp)
static FEYNMAN_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>, f64); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), 0.5),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), 0.5),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), 0.5),
]);

// ENGINE 75: Tier-0 Feynman Path Integral & Least-Action Quantum Trajectory Optimizer
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = FEYNMAN_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_s, prev_coh) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_s, prev_coh);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let book_bias = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);

        // Evaluate Euclidean Action S_k = Integral (T_kinetic + V_potential) dt across 3 virtual trajectories [-1.5, 0.0, +1.5]
        let virtual_paths = Vector3::new(-1.5, 0.0, 1.5);
        let hbar_eff = 0.85;

        let inst_actions = virtual_paths.map(|v_path| {
            let kinetic_dev = 0.5 * (ret_bps - v_path).powi(2);
            let potential_barrier = (1.0 - (v_path.signum() * book_bias)).max(0.1) * 0.8;
            (kinetic_dev + potential_barrier).clamp(0.05, 20.0)
        });

        let action_s = (prev_s * 0.75) + (inst_actions * 0.25);

        // Wick-Rotated Feynman Propagator Weights: W_k = exp(-S_k / hbar) & Constructive Phase Interference
        let prop_weights = action_s.map(|s| (-s / hbar_eff).exp());
        let partition_k = prop_weights.sum().max(1e-6);
        let path_probs = prop_weights / partition_k;

        // Least-Action Trajectory Dominance & Quantum Phase Coherence
        let optimal_dir = path_probs[2] - path_probs[0];
        let max_prob = path_probs.max();
        let coherence = (prev_coh * 0.72) + (max_prob * 0.28);

        let feynman_regime = if coherence > 0.52 && optimal_dir > 0.25 {
            "🚀 [FEYNMAN_LEAST_ACTION_BULL_PROPAGATOR]"
        } else if coherence > 0.52 && optimal_dir < -0.25 {
            "🩸 [FEYNMAN_LEAST_ACTION_BEAR_PROPAGATOR]"
        } else if path_probs[1] > 0.50 {
            "⚖️ [FEYNMAN_STATIONARY_CLASSICAL_EXTREMUM]"
        } else {
            "🌀 [FEYNMAN_DESTRUCTIVE_PHASE_INTERFERENCE]"
        };

        println!(
            "🧬 [E75:{}] S_MIN:{:.2} | COH_K:{:.1}% | DIR_PATH:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol,
            action_s.min(),
            coherence * 100.0,
            optimal_dir,
            feynman_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, action_s, coherence);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_76_ricci_curvature_flow.rs =====
use nalgebra::{Matrix2, Vector2};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, metric_g_mat2, curvature_vec[scalar_r, flow_dir])
static RICCI_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Matrix2<f64>, Vector2<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Matrix2::new(1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0), Vector2::new(0.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Matrix2::new(1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0), Vector2::new(0.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Matrix2::new(1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0), Vector2::new(0.0, 0.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 76: Tier-0 Ricci Flow & Riemannian Manifold Scalar Curvature Singularity Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = RICCI_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_g, prev_c) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_g, prev_c);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let book_imb = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);

        // Construct instantaneous Riemannian Metric Tensor perturbation from price-liquidity strain
        let g11_obs = (1.0 + ret_bps * ret_bps * 0.35).clamp(0.20, 10.0);
        let g22_obs = (1.0 + book_imb * book_imb * 1.50).clamp(0.20, 10.0);
        let g12_obs = (ret_bps * book_imb * 0.45).clamp(-1.5, 1.5);
        let obs_g = Matrix2::new(g11_obs, g12_obs, g12_obs, g22_obs);

        // Estimate Scalar Curvature R from metric determinant contraction & trace distortion
        let det_g = obs_g.determinant().max(0.05);
        let trace_g = obs_g.trace();
        let inst_r = ((trace_g - 2.0) / det_g) - (prev_g.determinant() - det_g);
        let scalar_r = ((prev_c[0] * 0.76) + (inst_r * 0.24)).clamp(-5.0, 10.0);

        // Hamilton-Perelman Normalized Ricci Flow step: dg_ij / dt = -2 * R_ij (where R_ij = 0.5 * R * g_ij in 2D)
        let dt = 0.12;
        let ricci_step = prev_g * (-scalar_r * dt);
        let mut next_g = (prev_g * 0.82) + (obs_g * 0.18) + ricci_step;
        next_g[(0, 0)] = next_g[(0, 0)].clamp(0.20, 10.0);
        next_g[(1, 1)] = next_g[(1, 1)].clamp(0.20, 10.0);

        let flow_dir = (prev_c[1] * 0.72) + (((ret_bps * 0.6) + (book_imb * 1.2)) * 0.28);
        let next_c = Vector2::new(scalar_r, flow_dir);

        let ricci_regime = if scalar_r > 1.35 && flow_dir > 0.30 {
            "⚡ [RICCI_NECK_PINCH_SINGULARITY_BULL_BURST]"
        } else if scalar_r > 1.35 && flow_dir < -0.30 {
            "🩸 [RICCI_NECK_PINCH_SINGULARITY_BEAR_BURST]"
        } else if scalar_r < -0.45 {
            "🌌 [RICCI_HYPERBOLIC_EXPANSION_COOLING]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [RIEMANNIAN_FLAT_EUCLIDEAN_METRIC]"
        };

        println!(
            "🕳️ [E76:{}] R_SCALAR:{:+.3} | det(g):{:.3} | FLOW:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_c[0], det_g, next_c[1], ricci_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_g, next_c);
    }
}

===== FILE: ./src/engine_77_navier_stokes_turbulence.rs =====
use nalgebra::Vector3;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, ns_vec[vel_u, reynolds_re, vorticity_omega])
static NAVIER_STOKES_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 100.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 100.0, 0.0)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 100.0, 0.0)),
]);

// ENGINE 77: Tier-0 Navier-Stokes Fluid Turbulence, Reynolds Number & Vortex Shedding Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = NAVIER_STOKES_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_ns) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_ns);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let rho_density = (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0).ln().clamp(0.5, 10.0);

        // Fluid Pressure Gradient -grad(P) from bid/ask wall imbalance & Dynamic Viscosity mu
        let neg_grad_p = ((bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1)) * 1.5;
        let mu_viscosity = (1.8 / (ret_bps.abs() + 0.5)).clamp(0.10, 3.5);

        // Navier-Stokes 1D Advection-Diffusion step: du/dt = -u*(du/dx) - (1/rho)*grad(P) + nu*laplacian(u)
        let u_prev = prev_ns[0];
        let du_advect = -0.15 * u_prev * (ret_bps - u_prev).abs();
        let viscous_laplacian = (mu_viscosity / rho_density) * (ret_bps - u_prev);
        let u_next = ((u_prev * 0.65) + ((ret_bps + (neg_grad_p / rho_density) + du_advect + viscous_laplacian) * 0.35)).clamp(-15.0, 15.0);

        // Reynolds Number Re = (rho * |u| * L) / mu & Shear Vorticity Curl omega = du_y / dx
        let inst_re = ((rho_density * u_next.abs().max(0.05) * 120.0) / mu_viscosity).clamp(10.0, 5000.0);
        let re_num = (prev_ns[1] * 0.78) + (inst_re * 0.22);
        let vorticity = (prev_ns[2] * 0.72) + (((u_next - u_prev) * neg_grad_p) * 0.28);

        let next_ns = Vector3::new(u_next, re_num, vorticity);

        let ns_regime = if re_num > 450.0 && u_next > 0.45 {
            "🌪️ [NAVIER_STOKES_TURBULENT_VORTEX_BULL_JET]"
        } else if re_num > 450.0 && u_next < -0.45 {
            "🌪️ [NAVIER_STOKES_TURBULENT_VORTEX_BEAR_JET]"
        } else if re_num < 160.0 {
            "🌊 [NAVIER_STOKES_LAMINAR_VISCOUS_FLOW]"
        } else {
            "🌀 [KARMAN_VORTEX_STREET_TRANSITION]"
        };

        println!(
            "🌊 [E77:{}] Re_NUM:{:.0} | u_VEL:{:+.2} | ω_VORTEX:{:+.3} -> {}",
            symbol, next_ns[1], next_ns[0], next_ns[2], ns_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_ns);
    }
}

use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (last_price, k41_vec[macro_vel, micro_vel, epsilon_dissipation])
static KOLMOGOROV_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.2)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.2)),

// ENGINE 78: Tier-0 Kolmogorov K41 Turbulence -5/3 Energy Cascade & Dissipation Microscale Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = KOLMOGOROV_STATE.lock() {
        let (last_p, prev_k) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_k);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;

        // Multi-scale eddy velocities: Integral Macro-Scale Velocity vs Micro-Tick Velocity
        let v_macro = (prev_k[0] * 0.92) + (ret_bps * 0.08);
        let v_micro = (prev_k[1] * 0.55) + (ret_bps * 0.45);
        let dv_eddy = (v_micro - v_macro).abs().max(0.05);

        // Kolmogorov K41 Four-Fifths Law Energy Dissipation Rate: epsilon ~ (delta_v)^3 / L_scale
        let inst_eps = (dv_eddy.powi(3) * 0.45).clamp(0.01, 50.0);
        let epsilon = (prev_k[2] * 0.78) + (inst_eps * 0.22);

        // Kinematic Viscosity nu from passive book depth & Kolmogorov Microscale eta = (nu^3 / epsilon)^(1/4)
        let nu_visc = (1.5 / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0).ln().max(0.5)).clamp(0.15, 2.5);
        let eta_microscale = ((nu_visc.powi(3)) / epsilon).powf(0.25).clamp(0.05, 10.0);

        // K41 Wavenumber Spectrum Energy E(k) = C_k * epsilon^(2/3) * k^(-5/3) at high tick frequency
        let tke_spectrum = 1.5 * epsilon.powf(2.0 / 3.0);
        let next_k = Vector3::new(v_macro, v_micro, epsilon);

        let k41_regime = if epsilon > 1.40 && v_micro > 0.45 {
            "🌪️ [K41_INERTIAL_CASCADE_BULL_BURST]"
        } else if epsilon > 1.40 && v_micro < -0.45 {
            "🩸 [K41_INERTIAL_CASCADE_BEAR_FLUSH]"
        } else if eta_microscale > 1.60 {
            "🧊 [K41_VISCOUS_DISSIPATION_SUBRANGE_CALM]"
        } else {
            "🌀 [RICHARDSON_EDDY_ENERGY_TRANSFER]"
        };

        println!(
            "🌀 [E78:{}] ε_DISS:{:.3} | η_SCALE:{:.3} | E(k):{:.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_k[2], eta_microscale, tke_spectrum, k41_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_k);
    }
}

    } else {

        .get("mark_price")

        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

        return;
    }

        let (last_p, prev_m) = state[idx];

        if last_p <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_m);
            return;
        }

        let ret_bps = ((price - last_p) / last_p) * 10000.0;
        let plasma_rho = (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0).ln().clamp(0.25, 10.0);

        // Magnetic Field B (Orderbook Imbalance flux density) & Induction rate dB/dt
        let inst_b = ((bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1)) * 2.5;
        let magnetic_b = (prev_m[0] * 0.75) + (inst_b * 0.25);
        let db_dt = (magnetic_b - prev_m[0]).abs();

        // Alfvén Speed v_A = |B| / sqrt(mu_0 * rho)
        let inst_va = (magnetic_b.abs() / plasma_rho.sqrt()).clamp(0.05, 50.0);
        let alfven_va = (prev_m[1] * 0.82) + (inst_va * 0.18);

        // Sweet-Parker Magnetic Reconnection Electric Field E = eta*J + v x B (Sudden field line snap)
        let plasma_eta = (1.2 / (plasma_rho + 0.5)).clamp(0.1, 2.0);
        let inst_rec_e = (plasma_eta * db_dt) + (ret_bps.abs() * alfven_va * 0.05);
        let recon_e = (prev_m[2] * 0.70) + (inst_rec_e * 0.30);

        let next_m = Vector3::new(magnetic_b, alfven_va, recon_e);

        let mhd_regime = if recon_e > 1.80 && magnetic_b > 0.40 {
            "⚡ [MHD_MAGNETIC_RECONNECTION_BULL_JET]"
        } else if recon_e > 1.80 && magnetic_b < -0.40 {
            "🩸 [MHD_MAGNETIC_RECONNECTION_BEAR_JET]"
        } else if alfven_va > 3.50 {
            "🌌 [ALFVEN_SUPER_WAVE_PROPAGATION]"
        } else {
            "⚖️ [Mhd_MAGNETO_STATIC_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "☀️ [E79:{}] B_FLUX:{:+.2} | v_A:{:.2} | E_RECON:{:.3} -> {}",
            symbol, next_m[0], next_m[1], next_m[2], mhd_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_m);
    }
}

        anchor_p = (anchor_p * 0.92) + (price * 0.08);
        let color_imb = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);

        // Running Strong Coupling alpha_s(Q^2) = 1 / (b0 * ln(Q^2 / Lambda^2)): drops at high momentum (Asymptotic Freedom)
        let q_sq = (disp_bps * disp_bps + color_imb * color_imb + 1.5).max(1.5);
        let inst_alpha_s = (1.15 / q_sq.ln().max(0.35)).clamp(0.08, 2.50);
        let alpha_s = (prev_q[1] * 0.80) + (inst_alpha_s * 0.20);

        // Non-Abelian Gluon Gauge Potential A_mu & Self-Interaction Field Strength F_mu_nu = dA + g * (A x A)
        let inst_a = (disp_bps * 0.45) + (color_imb * 1.40);
        let gluon_a = (prev_q[0] * 0.72) + (inst_a * 0.28);
        let da_dt = gluon_a - prev_q[0];

        // Yang-Mills Lagrangian Action Density: L_YM = 0.25 * F^2 (includes non-linear g * A^2 self-coupling)
        let f_tensor = da_dt + (alpha_s * gluon_a * color_imb);
        let inst_f2 = (0.25 * f_tensor * f_tensor).clamp(0.01, 30.0);
        let ym_action = (prev_q[2] * 0.75) + (inst_f2 * 0.25);

        let next_q = Vector3::new(gluon_a, alpha_s, ym_action);

        let qcd_regime = if ym_action > 1.25 && alpha_s < 0.45 && gluon_a > 0.35 {
            "⚡ [QCD_QUARK_GLUON_PLASMA_BULL_DECONFINEMENT]"
        } else if ym_action > 1.25 && alpha_s < 0.45 && gluon_a < -0.35 {
            "🩸 [QCD_QUARK_GLUON_PLASMA_BEAR_DECONFINEMENT]"
        } else if alpha_s > 0.95 {
            "🔗 [QCD_COLOR_CONFINEMENT_FLUX_TUBE_LOCK]"
        } else {
            "⚛️ [YANG_MILLS_GAUGE_VACUUM_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "⚛️ [E80:{}] α_s:{:.3} | L_YM:{:.3} | A_GLUON:{:+.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_q[1], next_q[2], next_q[0], qcd_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (anchor_p, next_q);
    }

use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Mutex;

// State per asset (BTC=0, ETH=1, SOL=2): (gravity_center_p, gr_vec[r_schwarzschild, time_dilation, escape_ratio])
static RELATIVITY_STATE: Mutex<[(f64, Vector3<f64>); 3]> = Mutex::new([
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 1.0, 0.5)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 1.0, 0.5)),
    (0.0, Vector3::new(0.5, 1.0, 0.5)),
]);

// ENGINE 81: Tier-0 General Relativity, Einstein Field Curvature & Schwarzschild Event Horizon Engine
pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {
    if parsed.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) != Some("v2/ticker") {
        return;
    }

    let symbol = match parsed.get("symbol").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return,
    };

    let idx = if symbol.contains("BTC") {
        0
    } else if symbol.contains("ETH") {
        1
    } else if symbol.contains("SOL") {
        2
    } else {
        return;
    };

    let price = parsed
        .get("mark_price")
        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0.0);

    let quotes = parsed.get("quotes");
    let bid_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

    if price <= 0.0 {
        return;
    }

    if let Ok(mut state) = RELATIVITY_STATE.lock() {
        let (mut grav_center, prev_gr) = state[idx];

        if grav_center <= 0.0 {
            state[idx] = (price, prev_gr);
            return;
        }

        // Radial coordinate r (distance from liquidity mass center in bps) & Stress-Energy Mass M
        let signed_r = ((price - grav_center) / grav_center) * 10000.0;
        let radial_r = signed_r.abs().max(0.15);
        grav_center = (grav_center * 0.93) + (price * 0.07);

        let stress_mass_m = ((bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0).ln() * 0.45).clamp(0.20, 6.0);
        let c_speed = 2.20;

        // Schwarzschild Event Horizon Radius: r_s = 2 * G * M / c^2
        let inst_rs = ((2.0 * stress_mass_m) / (c_speed * c_speed)).clamp(0.10, 4.5);
        let r_s = (prev_gr[0] * 0.80) + (inst_rs * 0.20);

        // Gravitational Time Dilation factor: gamma_g = sqrt(max(1 - r_s / r, 0.01))
        let inst_dilation = (1.0 - (r_s / radial_r)).clamp(0.01, 1.0).sqrt();
        let time_dil = (prev_gr[1] * 0.75) + (inst_dilation * 0.25);

        // Escape Velocity v_esc = sqrt(2GM / r) & Kinetic Escape Ratio |v| / v_esc
        let v_esc = ((2.0 * stress_mass_m) / radial_r).sqrt().max(0.10);
        let book_thrust = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);
        let kinetic_v = (signed_r.abs() * 0.65) + (book_thrust.abs() * 1.15);
        let escape_ratio = (prev_gr[2] * 0.72) + ((kinetic_v / v_esc).clamp(0.05, 5.0) * 0.28);

        let next_gr = Vector3::new(r_s, time_dil, escape_ratio);

        let gr_regime = if escape_ratio > 1.35 && signed_r > 0.30 {
            "🚀 [RELATIVISTIC_ESCAPE_VELOCITY_BULL_BREAKOUT]"
        } else if escape_ratio > 1.35 && signed_r < -0.30 {
            "🩸 [RELATIVISTIC_ESCAPE_VELOCITY_BEAR_BREAKOUT]"
        } else if radial_r <= r_s * 1.15 || time_dil < 0.35 {
            "🕳️ [SCHWARZSCHILD_EVENT_HORIZON_GRAVITY_TRAP]"
        } else {
            "🌌 [EINSTEIN_GEODESIC_ORBIT_EQUILIBRIUM]"
        };

        println!(
            "🕳️ [E81:{}] r_s:{:.2} | γ_TIME:{:.2} | v/v_ESC:{:.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_gr[0], next_gr[1], next_gr[2], gr_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (grav_center, next_gr);
    }
}

pub fn process_tick(parsed: &Value) {

        .and_then(|p| p.as_str())

        .unwrap_or(0.0);

        .and_then(|q| q.get("bid_size"))
        .and_then(|b| b.as_str())

        .unwrap_or(1.0);
    let ask_sz = quotes
        .and_then(|q| q.get("ask_size"))
        .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1.0);

        return;
    }

            state[idx] = (price, prev_h);
            return;

        let book_imb = (bid_sz - ask_sz) / (bid_sz + ask_sz).max(0.1);

        // Effective Black Hole Liquidity Mass M from passive orderbook depth
        let inst_mass = ((bid_sz + ask_sz).max(1.0).ln() * 0.42).clamp(0.22, 6.0);
        let mass_m = (prev_h[0] * 0.78) + (inst_mass * 0.22);

        // Hawking Temperature T_H ~ 1 / M & Evaporation Luminosity L_H ~ 1 / M^2
        let inst_th = (0.85 / mass_m).clamp(0.05, 5.0);
        let temp_th = (prev_h[1] * 0.75) + (inst_th * 0.25);

        let shrink_boost = (-dm_dt).max(0.0) * 3.5;
        let inst_evap = ((0.75 / (mass_m * mass_m)) + shrink_boost).clamp(0.02, 20.0);
        let evap_flux = (prev_h[2] * 0.72) + (inst_evap * 0.28);

        // Bekenstein-Hawking Area Entropy S_BH ~ M^2
        let bh_entropy = 0.50 * mass_m * mass_m;
        let dir_drive = (ret_bps * 0.65) + (book_imb * 1.15);
        let next_h = Vector3::new(mass_m, temp_th, evap_flux);

        let hawk_regime = if evap_flux > 1.65 && dm_dt < -0.02 && dir_drive > 0.25 {
            "💥 [HAWKING_EVAPORATION_FLASH_BULL_ERUPTION]"
        } else if evap_flux > 1.65 && dm_dt < -0.02 && dir_drive < -0.25 {
            "🩸 [HAWKING_EVAPORATION_FLASH_BEAR_COLLAPSE]"
        } else if bh_entropy > 2.40 && temp_th < 0.45 {
            "🧊 [BEKENSTEIN_COLD_HORIZON_WALL_ABSORPTION]"
        } else {
            "☢️ [HAWKING_QUANTUM_VACUUM_RADIATION_DRIFT]"
        };

        println!(
            "☢️ [E82:{}] T_HAWK:{:.3} | L_EVAP:{:.3} | S_BH:{:.2} -> {}",
            symbol, next_h[1], next_h[2], bh_entropy, hawk_regime
        );

        state[idx] = (price, next_h);
    }
